The `STOP` file was set to `2026-11-13 15:25` (the 5-hour limit resets at 15:20 local); the keep-alive then starts the watcher, which relaunches Phase 13 on Opus, then Phase 11. To return to Fable after the weekly reset on 2026-11-19, delete `agent_model.txt`.

## Phase 14: sun-path timelapse videos -- 2026-11-13

### Editor restart for memory -- 2026-11-13 (Phase 14 session)

- Cause: free commit memory was 21.7 GB (79.1 GB limit, 57.4 GB committed; the editor alone held 23.7 GB), under the 30 GB rule. The editor was asked to close with `SystemLibrary.quit_editor()` and reopened on `/Game/Wieland1529/L_Site_M4`; free commit after closing was 47.3 GB.
- Note: the closing call came back "Canceled" because the agent server went down with the editor; this is expected, not a fault.
- Status: done; the server answered again about 40 seconds after reopening.

### Two copies of the shooting script ran at once -- 2026-11-13 (Phase 14 session)

- Error: three of the four first MP4 files (`roofdeck_summer`, `reardeck_summer`, `reardeck_winter`) failed to decode ("Invalid NAL unit size"), and every frame of those three days was logged twice.
- Cause: `run_all.sh` was first started as `nohup ... &` inside a tool call that then reported "completed"; the shell I assumed was killed kept running. I then started a second copy. Both copies wrote the same job file, the same frame folders and the same MP4 files, and their scripts ran interleaved inside the editor, so the sun and exposure of one could be changed under the other. The frames of those three days are not trustworthy even where they look fine.
- Fix: the three frame folders and their captioned copies were renamed to `*_suspect_doublerun` (nothing deleted) and the three MP4 files moved to `review/phase14/suspect/`; the three days were shot again from scratch with a single copy running. Check each new MP4 with `ffmpeg -v error -i file -f null -` (no output means clean) and look at the frame log for one line per frame.
- Lesson: start a long job once, with `run_in_background` on the tool call itself, never with `nohup &`; before starting a second copy, look at the process list.
- Status: fixed. The three days were shot again by a single run; all four final MP4 files decode with no `ffmpeg` errors, have 240 frames at 24 frames a second (10.0 s), and every frame log has one line per frame. The suspect frames and files are kept under `*_suspect_doublerun` and `review/phase14/suspect/` (not committed).

## Phase 13: the proposed hotel at 1520-1528 N. Wells ("The Duke of Wells") -- 2026-11-13 12:30 to 16:00 (two sessions; journal `docs\unreal\1529_wieland\phase13_results.md`)

Errors and surprises of the phase, each with cause, fix and status. "Jn" is the journal line.

- J1, the kickoff's premise was wrong: it placed the hotel across a public alley behind 1529 (east). Cause: the press describes the site from Wells Street; nobody had checked the parcels. Fix: county parcels and the PD 1335 boundary map show no alley; the development land adjoins 1529's south lot line, so the hotel is the south and south-east neighbour and Home 3 stands 1.7 ft from the lot line. Recorded in `proposed_hotel.md` 1.1. Status: fixed; for the owner to confirm.
- J1, the press says two homes, the PD three. Fix: the PD is the legal record; three modelled. Status: for the owner.
- J4, the first session stopped at 13:09 with the weekly Fable usage limit (see the supervisor's entry above) while J4 was PENDING. Phase 14 ran in between and restarted the editor at 14:00. Fix: the resumed session (15:31, Opus) inspected what existed (the probe results `measure_traces.json` and the eight model views were complete), reused them, and only made the comparison sheets. Status: handled.
- J4, the comparison sheets contain copies of the publishers' renderings. Fix: kept on disk, not committed, as for the renderings. Status: for the owner.
- J5, the side-yard camera (SY) was first placed with the sign of north reversed and too close (inside the side yard, looking at a wall). Cause: site feet north map to level -X; the sign was typed wrongly. Fix: placed 30 ft west of the lot line in Wieland Street, 3 ft south of 1529's south line; test picture checked by eye before the full run. Status: fixed.
- J6, the first run of the shadow line tests counted objects that are hidden (Revit's neighbour massing and slab stand-ins, hidden for every render) as casting shadow. Cause: a hidden object keeps its collision shape and still stops a line test. Fix: `shadow_traces.py` steps over objects that do not draw (Revit stand-ins and objects without a visible mesh), except the three existing objects of the hide list, which it counts only for the existing option. The new-shade numbers changed by up to 3.5 points (12:00: 4.4 to 7.9 %). Status: fixed. Lesson: line tests see collision, not visibility.
- J6, the shadow sheet first failed on file names. Cause: `wieland_truth.render` puts the suffix after "realtime" (`S8_realtime_0900.0000.png`). Fix: names corrected in `shadow_sheet.py` and in the docstring of `shadow_pics.py`. Status: fixed.
- Whole resumed session, free commit memory 29.0 to 29.3 GB, just under the 30 GB line, the editor holding 17.1 to 17.5 GB. Fix (workaround, as Phase 11 J3): only light work was done (real-time pictures, line tests, one profile recording), with the memory log running; no path-traced picture was made. Status: handled; no crash.
- Whole phase, the kickoff says the hotel "stands on the east side" and asks for the morning shadow. Finding: in August the morning sun is east of the hotel's bearing from 1529, so the morning shadow does not reach the lot (0.2 % at 09:00); the shadow comes around midday (up to 8.2 % at 13:00). Status: reported.
- Closing, `PROJECT_MEMORY.md` was found rewritten with Windows (CRLF) line endings and with the Phase 14 section not committed. Cause: the Phase 14 session's editor. Fix: line endings put back to LF (content unchanged, checked with `git diff --ignore-cr-at-eol`), then this section added; the Phase 14 section is committed with it. Status: fixed.
- Closing, the working copy still shows `profiles\2026-11-12_M6\memory_log_phase8.csv` and Phase 11's scripts and job files as changed. Cause: other phases' processes and sessions. Fix: left alone, not this phase's files. Status: reported to the owner.
- J6/J9, the shadow test's points were first chosen with a different filter from the test itself: the line from above stopped on the hidden Revit grade, and 181 of 820 lines passed through 1529's roof and landed indoors (ceilings, furniture, partitions). Cause: 1529 has roof parts that are not drawn and have no collision; hidden objects keep collision. Fix: `first_surface()` steps over objects that do not draw, and indoor points are left out; shares are now of 639 outdoor points. Peak new shade from the hotel went from 8.2 % to 4.9 % (13:00). Found by the closing review. Status: fixed.
- J6, the plan pictures (S8 camera) show 1529 open from above, with rooms and furniture visible. Cause: roof parts of 1529 are not drawn in the saved scene; this was so before Phase 13 (the "existing" pictures show the same). Fix: none in this phase. Status: open, for the owner and Phase 11 (affects plan pictures and line tests, not the street and deck views).
- J9, the Revit neighbour massing stand-in has a 30 ft block on the hotel's land (1525 N. Wieland). Its mesh component is switched off for drawing in the saved scene, so it never shows and was left alone. Status: recorded.
- J9, the hotel's materials had no recorded grazing-light check (J3's step title named it, its outcome did not). Fix: close views under an 8-degree raking sun (exposure EV 11.5 for those pictures only, put back to 14.644 and read back) and under the review sun, `review\phase13\grazing\`. The first try at the fixed daylight exposure came out too dark to judge, and its penthouse camera stood above the panel; both corrected in the second try. Result: no seams or repeats; the brick's mortar joints have shallow relief. Status: done; shallow joints listed for the owner.
- J9, the frame-time comparison was first described as "costs nothing measurable". Cause: both runs had the hotel level loaded, so they measure drawing, not memory. Fix: the wording now separates the two and gives the assets' size on disk (72 MB) as a stand-in for memory. Status: fixed.
- Closing, two appends of long text through a shell here-document failed with "unexpected EOF while looking for matching quote". Cause: the session's shell tool mis-parses long here-documents that hold backticks and quotes. Fix: the text is written to a file with the editor tool and appended by a small Python script. Status: workaround in use.
- Closing, the Phase 13 memory logger (process 3812) was stopped at 16:03. Phase 11's logger (process 23580) was left running.

## Phase 11: packaging and delivery -- 2026-11-13 08:28 onward (several sessions; journal `docs\unreal\1529_wieland\phase11_results.md`)

Errors and surprises of the phase, each with cause, fix and status. "Jn" is the journal line. The first sessions' errors were not written here at the time (J2 says they were); they are filled in from the journal by the session that resumed at 16:12.

- J1, the editor (process 16168) vanished between 08:59:52 and 09:00:53 while the path-traced still D2 was rendering. Cause: not known; the log ends inside the render and no crash record was written. Fix: the watcher restarted the editor at 10:25; D2 was rendered again at 10:46 (644 s) with `r.RayTracing.Nanite.Mode` 0. Status: handled; cause open.
- J2, the flat lot ground (`SG_lot_placeholder`) and the far-city apron (`FAR_CityStandIn`) passed through the building's lower level, so a camera there saw gravel 9 cm under the eye. Cause: both are flat sheets made in Phases 4 and 7 without a hole for the basement. Fix: cut copies `SM_W1529_LotGround_Cut` and `SM_W1529_FarCity_Cut` under `/Game/Wieland1529/Delivery/Meshes/`, swapped onto the two objects (recorded swap; old meshes untouched). Status: fixed.
- J2, two tool errors while cutting: a selection call returned a pair, not a list; and `post_edit_change` does not exist in the Python interface. Fix: unpack the pair; leave the change notice to `set_editor_property`. Status: fixed.
- J3, the 30 GB free-commit line could not be reached: a freshly started editor with `L_Site_M4` open leaves 27.7 to 28.4 GB free of 79.1 GB. Cause: other programs this session may not close (browsers, the Claude desktop program and its helpers, Windows) hold about 30 GB. Fix (workaround): stills, the animation and packaging run with the line lowered to 26 GB, one at a time, with the memory log every 3 s; each peak is written down (a path-traced still raises the editor by about 10.5 GB; lowest free seen 19.3 GB, no crash). Status: workaround in use; for the owner.
- J5, the first animation test frames were three stops too bright. Cause: a cine camera puts its own aperture (f/2.8 unless set) into the exposure, while the lighting level's exposure is written for f/8. Fix: `seq_build.py` sets the camera's aperture to f/8 and motion blur to 0.2; both sequences were rebuilt and saved at 12:21 and 12:22 and read back. Status: fixed.
- J5, the owner interrupted the phase at 12:30 for Phase 13 (the proposed hotel), which changes eight of the twelve stills. Fix: the eight are re-rendered with the hotel on (J6); the earlier eight are kept in `delivery\stills_prehotel\`. Status: in hand.
- 16:12, the working copy showed `profiles\2026-11-12_M6\memory_log_phase8.csv` with 5,823 lines appended up to 08:56 this morning. Cause: a memory logger started during Phase 11's first session wrote to the Phase 8 file. Fix: left uncommitted and untouched (not deleted); the Phase 11 logger writes to `review\phase11\memory_log_phase11.csv`. Status: reported to the owner.
- 2026-11-14 J7, the animation's runs of 150 frames joined badly: the first frame of each run was 20 to 35 % darker than the frame before it (frame 150 against 149, 300 against 299). Cause: the 64 warm-up frames before each run do not let Lumen's bounced light settle; frame 300 rendered alone reads 33.1 (mean grey) with 64 warm-up frames, 47.2 with 512, 51.3 with 1,024, against 50.9 in the continuous run. Fix: each move is rendered as one run of 900 frames with 1,024 warm-up frames; the 397 frames made with 64 were moved (not deleted) to `D:\Wieland1529_delivery\animation_frames\test\superseded_w64\`. Status: fixed for the triplex move (900 frames, one run, no join); penthouse rendering.
- 2026-11-14 J7, the memory log stopped at 05:06. Cause: `tools\memory_log.ps1` runs for 110 minutes unless told otherwise, and the logger of the earlier session reached its end. Fix: started again for 24 hours at 3-second steps; a second program (`review\phase11\scripts\floor_watch.ps1`) cancels any render if free commit memory falls under 15 GB. Status: fixed.
- 2026-11-14, this session runs in the editor's own agent panel: its process is a child of the editor (UnrealEditor -> cmd -> node -> claude). Closing and reopening the editor, which the rules ask for when free commit memory is under 30 GB, would end the session itself. Fix: the editor is not restarted; memory is given back by garbage collection (05:37: free commit rose from 29.3 to 30.2 GB and the penthouse run started) and, for packaging and profiling, by opening an empty level in the editor instead of closing it. Status: workaround in use; for the owner and the supervisor (an editor restart must be done from outside the panel).
- 2026-11-14, text passed to Python through the shell lost backslashes (\a, \t, \f became control characters) twice: once in the J7 journal line, once in this file. Cause: the shell tool collapses doubled backslashes before a here-document reaches Python. Fix: `review\phase11\scripts\fix_controls.py` repaired both files; text with Windows paths is now written with the file tools, not through the shell. Status: fixed.
- 2026-11-14 J11, packaging stopped at 06:45 in its build step with "No valid Visual C++ toolchain was found (minimum version 14.38.33130, preferred 14.50.35717)" (exit code 6). Cause: the project counts as a code project for packaging because enabled engine plugins have game-side parts (SunPosition first), so a small game program must be compiled; Unreal's build tool finds compilers only through Visual Studio's installation registry (the COM "SetupConfiguration" interface), and on this machine that registry lists no installation at all (`vswhere -all -prerelease -products *` returns nothing; one record folder `C:\ProgramData\Microsoft\VisualStudio\Packages\_Instances\662d3a74` exists), although three complete MSVC compilers are on disk: 14.50.35717 (`C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft Visual Studio\18\BuildTools`), 14.44.35207 (2022 BuildTools and 2022 Community), with the Windows SDK 10.0.26100. Fix (workaround, nothing installed, no registry or Windows setting changed): the build tool's "AutoSDK" route, a folder `E:\Temp\w1529_autosdk\HostWin64\Win64\VS2026\14.50.35717` and `...\VS2022\14.44.35207` made of directory links (junctions) to the installed compilers, with `UE_SDKS_ROOT` set only for the packaging process (`review\phase11\scripts\package.ps1 -AutoSdkRoot`). The second attempt at 06:48 found "Visual Studio 14.50.35729 toolchain" and compiled. Status: worked around; for the owner: Visual Studio's installer records are broken (a repair of Visual Studio from its installer would restore them; that is a change to the machine and is the owner's to make). DefaultEngine.ini was put back after the failed attempt (SHA-256 equal to the copy).

- Owner: "remove the hotel replace with lot we had before. then continue on, remove all hotel work".
- Done by the supervisor in the repository:
  - removed `KICKOFF_PHASE13.md`, `phase13_results.md`, `proposed_hotel.md`, `review\phase13\`, `profiles\2026-11-13_P13\` and `tools\wieland_hotel.py`, plus untracked scratch;
  - removed the Phase 13 section of `PLAN.md` and the four hotel rows of `asset_downloads.csv`.
  - All of it remains in git history at `26b8b768`. The earlier Phase 13 entries in this file are kept as history.
- Queued:
  - Phase 15 (Opus) removes the hotel level, its assets, the `Neighbour development` variant list and the overrides from the Unreal project, restores the existing buildings, and moves `E:\Wieland1529_site_data\proposed_hotel\` to the Recycle Bin.
  - Phase 16 (Sonnet) redoes the four timelapses without the hotel.
  - Phase 11 then re-renders any still made with the hotel and continues.
- A first attempt to strip the hotel rows from `asset_downloads.csv` by splitting on CRLF also deleted unrelated rows, because the file mixes line endings. It was caught in the diff, reverted, and redone by splitting on LF; only the 4 hotel rows went.
- 23:45: the owner cancelled the timelapse redo ("dont re do timelapses"). Phase 16 was removed from the queue and its kickoff file deleted; the Phase 14 videos, made with the hotel, stay as they are. Queue: Phase 15, then Phase 11.

## Phase 15: the withdrawn hotel removed from the project -- 2026-11-13 23:41 onward (journal `docs\unreal\1529_wieland\phase15_results.md`)

Errors and surprises of the phase, each with cause, fix and status. "Jn" is the journal line.

- J2, `VariantManagerLibrary.get_value_bool` returned False for every recorded value of every list (both options of "Surroundings" "matched" the scene on exactly the 54 objects hidden at the time). Cause: the Python call does not read the recorded value of a captured Visible property in UE 5.9. Fix: the state before Phase 13 was worked out from behaviour instead: the option already on was switched on again and the objects read back, and the live visibility of every other object of each list was counted. Status: worked around; do not use `get_value_bool` to read a list.
- J4, the kickoff's name pattern `SM_W1529_PH_*` also matches 14 meshes `SM_W1529_PH_<size>` in `/Game/Wieland1529/Interiors/Generated/Placeholders/` ("PH" = placeholder, furniture stand-ins used by the four interior levels). Cause: two phases used the same short prefix. Fix: only assets in the hotel's own folder `/Game/Wieland1529/Site/ProposedHotel/` and the level `L_ProposedHotel` were deleted, after a referencer check; the 14 placeholders were kept untouched and still exist. Status: avoided.
- J4, `EditorAssetLibrary.delete_asset` on the level `/Game/Wieland1529/Site/L_ProposedHotel` returned True twice but left `L_ProposedHotel.umap` on disk (the asset registry dropped it; the second call loaded the file again, with missing-mesh warnings because the meshes were already gone). Cause: the editor's undo history was still open while it purged ("Non zero active count in UTransBuffer::Reset", reason "Delete Selected Item"), so the world package could not be released at the moment the files are removed. Fix: with the package out of memory and nothing using it, the file was moved to the Windows Recycle Bin (`D:\$RECYCLE.BIN\...\$R98MWBV.umap`), not deleted outright; the registry, the folder and a byte search of every `.umap`/`.uasset` show no hotel left. Status: fixed. Next time delete a level with nothing else in the same call, then collect garbage and check the file.

### Watcher never re-armed after the panel agent -- 2026-11-13 23:43 to 2026-11-14 04:54 (supervising session)

- Error: the owner's panel agent finished Phase 15 (23:59) and resumed Phase 11 (00:05). It stopped at about 00:07, before J7. The watcher stayed paused, so nothing ran for about 4 hours 47 minutes.
- Cause: `arm_after_panel.ps1` ignored the session record `b7e27df1…jsonl`, assuming it belonged only to the stopped headless agent. The panel agent resumed that same record, so the script never saw it and never lifted the `STOP` pause.
- Fix: the script now counts any session record written after it starts, including resumed ones and those in subfolders. Re-armed at 04:55. A new panel prompt, `KICKOFF_PANEL_PHASE11_RESUME.md`, resumes Phase 11 at J7.
- Prevention: never exclude an earlier session record when detecting a panel agent; the panel may resume it.
- Status: fixed; to be confirmed when the panel agent starts.
